六月の猛幽=I appreciate your request, but I should note that "六月の猛幽" doesn't appear to be standard Japanese that forms a clear meaning. 

Breaking it down:
- 六月 (rokugatsu) = June
- 猛 (takesi/mō) = fierce, violent
- 幽 (yū) = faint, dim, ghostly

A literal translation would be: "June's Fierce Ghosts" or "Fierce Dimness of June"

However, this may be:
- A proper name/title
